one immutable review binding with exact append-only native continuation custody\\\",\\\"body\\\":\\\"Date: 2026-11-06\\\\nApprove the refined EI429 design in post1216138 for delegated implementation; the initial replacement-ledger/index-change concept in1216130 is rejected. Keep one immutable logical binding and its original native/ADV tuple, and preserve replacement history and owner uniqueness. Reuse maintained session_ports for transcript conversion and persistence. A narrow append-only binding-to-port custody relation is justified only for blind-review authority: generic mutable port metadata cannot by itself grant posting/runtime authority. Reserve it under the existing recruiter lease and plan lock; pin workspace, binding/activation, same owner, source incarnation, port, exact prepared target and unchanged confinement. Use one transaction-local active-endpoint resolver for resume, post, runtime admission and cutoff. Same owner alone is insufficient: posting must verify authenticated native/ADV provenance against the resolved endpoint; stale source posting and old runtime leases fail after transfer. While a launch or transfer is uncertain, keep the reservation and deny duplicate launch/authority; never release uncertainty into an ordinary retry. AND part_kind='tool_use' AND ts>=c.at AND ts<c.\"endAt\"),matched AS MATERIALIZED (SELECT * FROM native n WHERE EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM jsonb_array_elements_text(c.paths)f(path) WHERE strpos(n.text,f.path)>0) AND NOT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM jsonb_to_recordset(c.\"savedCandidates\")k(source_kind text,session_id text,part_idx int) WHERE k.source_kind=n.source_kind AND k.session_id=n.session_id AND k.part_idx=n.part_idx)), selected AS (SELECT * FROM matched ORDER BY ts,part_idx LIMIT 2000) SELECT (SELECT count(*)::int FROM matched) AS matched_rows,coalesce((SELECT jsonb_agg(jsonb_build_object('source_kind',s.source_kind,'session_id',s.session_id,'part_idx',s.part_idx,'ts',s.ts,'tool_name',s.tool_name,'text',s.text,'followingResult',r.part) ORDER BY s.ts,s.part_idx) FROM selected s LEFT JOIN LATERAL (SELECT jsonb_build_object('part_idx',p.part_idx,'ts',p.ts,'tool_name',p.tool_name,'text',p.text) AS part FROM harness_shared.session_turn_parts p WHERE p.workspace_id='default' AND p.source_kind=s.source_kind AND p.session_id=s.session_id AND p.part_idx=s.part_idx+1 AND p.part_kind='tool_result' LIMIT 1)r ON true),'[]'::jsonb) AS candidates)p A NULL operand makes a comparison UNKNOWN rather than false, so such a row satisfies NO branch — it is silently absent from every bucket AND from the filtered total, while the numbers still come back well-formed (WI-10002034: a measured census reported 96/0/2 while 94 rows fell through every FILTER, and re-asking it as an exhaustive partition INVERTED the conclusion). A scalar cannot show you this, because the wrong answer is shaped exactly like the right one. total:\nSELECT\n       (owner=c.claimer) AS d1,\n       (part_kind='tool_use') AS d2,\n       (ts>=c.at) AS d3,\n       count(*) AS row_count\n  FROM jsonb_to_recordset\n WHERE
